# Third-party notices

TOWER Bridge contains the following software and data by others, each used under the license named beside it. Where a component offers a choice of licenses, the one TOWER Bridge uses is named. The license texts follow each list.

This file is generated by `npm run notices`; do not edit it by hand.

## Fonts, icons and map data

- Inter, the app's text font: OFL-1.1, https://github.com/rsms/inter
- Roboto Mono, the font of addresses and code: OFL-1.1, https://github.com/googlefonts/robotomono
- Barlow Condensed Bold, the digits of the tray icon on Windows: OFL-1.1, https://github.com/jpt/barlow
- Lucide, icons: ISC, partly MIT (Feather), https://lucide.dev
- world-atlas 2.0.2, the country outlines of the GPS map, from Natural Earth: ISC, https://github.com/topojson/world-atlas
- Natural Earth 5.1.2, the lakes, rivers and towns of the GPS map: Public domain, https://www.naturalearthdata.com
- Wikidata, the Czech and Slovak town names of the GPS map: CC0-1.0, https://www.wikidata.org
